commit 971cfd16d8744e70629eca11683c77d69157d9b2
Author: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>
Date:   Sun Feb 15 10:39:28 2026 +0000

    Speed up spell generation: skip Planner for QUICK tier, use gpt-4o-mini for STANDARD
    
    Three optimizations to reduce spell generation latency:
    
    1. QUICK tier: Skip the LLM planner entirely and use a deterministic plan
       built from WORKING_TYPES block sequences. The planner just picks blocks
       and anchors — for QUICK spells the working type already defines this.
       Saves 5-12 seconds per spell.
    
    2. STANDARD tier: Switch planner model from gpt-4o to gpt-4o-mini.
       The planner outputs structured JSON (block sequences, canon anchors),
       not creative prose — gpt-4o-mini handles this well and is 3-5x faster.
       DEEP tier keeps gpt-4o for better planning quality.
    
    3. QUICK tier: Deterministic plan runs instantly (<1ms) so the only
       LLM wait is Archivist + Writer (2 calls instead of 3).
    
    Expected timing improvements:
    - QUICK: ~25s → ~15s (planner eliminated)
    - STANDARD: ~40s → ~32s (faster planner model)
    - DEEP: unchanged (keeps gpt-4o planner)
    
    https://claude.ai/code/session_01TmD2vz5VWjqdoHTUHWgzyY

commit 5bcb95b2f5ad9c5a51aea32331a89cc729fc2de7
Author: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>
Date:   Sun Feb 15 10:23:45 2026 +0000

    Fix Theresa spell generation: add working types, increase token budget, fix JSON repair
    
    Theresa's spells were consistently failing QA validation with only 3 of 7
    blocks generated. Root causes:
    
    1. Theresa had only 2 working types (vs 5-6 for other guides) — added
       veil_breaking, genealogical_mapping, red_thread_working, bird_field_log
    2. JSON repair function only preserved first 3000 chars of truncated output,
       losing most blocks — increased to 8000 chars
    3. STANDARD tier writer_tokens (2500) was too tight for evidence_card-heavy
       blocks — increased to 3200
    4. Writer prompt always mentioned bird_oracle for Theresa even when working
       type didn't include it — made guide-specific blocks working-type-aware
    5. Default BLOCK_TEMPLATE for Theresa required 9 blocks (including materials
       and bird_oracle) — reduced to 7 core blocks
    6. truth_seeking working type had 9 required blocks — reduced to 7
    7. QA validation now working-type-aware for Theresa specialty blocks
    
    https://claude.ai/code/session_01TmD2vz5VWjqdoHTUHWgzyY

    Add detailed fix instructions for spell generation pipeline bugs
    
    The critical issue is a blocks format mismatch: pipeline_blocks.py

    evidence_card), but SpellBlockRenderer.jsx expects an array of objects

commit 971cfd16d8744e70629eca11683c77d69157d9b2
Author: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>
Date:   Sun Feb 15 10:39:28 2026 +0000

    Speed up spell generation: skip Planner for QUICK tier, use gpt-4o-mini for STANDARD
    
    Three optimizations to reduce spell generation latency:
    
    1. QUICK tier: Skip the LLM planner entirely and use a deterministic plan
       built from WORKING_TYPES block sequences. The planner just picks blocks
       and anchors — for QUICK spells the working type already defines this.
       Saves 5-12 seconds per spell.
    
    2. STANDARD tier: Switch planner model from gpt-4o to gpt-4o-mini.
       The planner outputs structured JSON (block sequences, canon anchors),
       not creative prose — gpt-4o-mini handles this well and is 3-5x faster.
       DEEP tier keeps gpt-4o for better planning quality.
    
    3. QUICK tier: Deterministic plan runs instantly (<1ms) so the only
       LLM wait is Archivist + Writer (2 calls instead of 3).
    
    Expected timing improvements:
    - QUICK: ~25s → ~15s (planner eliminated)
    - STANDARD: ~40s → ~32s (faster planner model)
    - DEEP: unchanged (keeps gpt-4o planner)
    
    https://claude.ai/code/session_01TmD2vz5VWjqdoHTUHWgzyY

commit 5bcb95b2f5ad9c5a51aea32331a89cc729fc2de7
Author: Claude <noreply@anthropic.com>
Date:   Sun Feb 15 10:23:45 2026 +0000

    Fix Theresa spell generation: add working types, increase token budget, fix JSON repair
    
    Theresa's spells were consistently failing QA validation with only 3 of 7
    blocks generated. Root causes:
    
    1. Theresa had only 2 working types (vs 5-6 for other guides) — added
       veil_breaking, genealogical_mapping, red_thread_working, bird_field_log
    2. JSON repair function only preserved first 3000 chars of truncated output,
       losing most blocks — increased to 8000 chars
    3. STANDARD tier writer_tokens (2500) was too tight for evidence_card-heavy
       blocks — increased to 3200
    4. Writer prompt always mentioned bird_oracle for Theresa even when working
       type didn't include it — made guide-specific blocks working-type-aware
    5. Default BLOCK_TEMPLATE for Theresa required 9 blocks (including materials
       and bird_oracle) — reduced to 7 core blocks
    6. truth_seeking working type had 9 required blocks — reduced to 7
    7. QA validation now working-type-aware for Theresa specialty blocks
